    These are my collection of some K-Pop events performance (until 2025).

    Some events performance in the folder have different broadcast versions —if you’re the type to watch those in detail.

    Usually in the WEB-DL version, since it was a Live Broadcast, so it didn't manually add audience cheers yet and camera only focus on the artists. Unlike the TV rebroadcast version, they added audience cheers mid-performance, making the music difficult to hear clearly, and the camera sometimes focused on the audience. Due to broadcast schedule, the TV station rebroadcast version is shorter than the live stream version. A lot of the stage performances and the talk sessions were shortened or cut.

    Thanks and Credit To:
    Original Capper     that's in the filename
    Compiler or Secondary Source: KpopBuzzHub, Sharing Korea Torrent, curiouschae, thaovox, etc.
